```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.graph

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Matrix
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.graphics.PointF
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.ScaleGestureDetector
import android.view.View
import androidx.core.graphics.toColorInt
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.GraphMacroScenario
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.ScenarioEdge
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.ScenarioNode
import java.util.UUID
import kotlin.math.hypot

@SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility")
class GraphCanvasView(context: Context) : View(context) {

    var scenario: GraphMacroScenario? = null
        private set

    var onScenarioModified: (() -> Unit)? = null
    var onNodeSelected: ((ScenarioNode) -> Unit)? = null

    private val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
    private fun dpF(v: Float) = v * dm.density

    private val canvasMatrix = Matrix()
    private val inverseMatrix = Matrix()
    private var scaleFactor = 1.0f
    private var panX = 40f
    private var panY = 100f

    private val nodeWidth get() = dpF(180f)
    private val headerHeight get() = dpF(26f)
    private val portRadius get() = dpF(7f)
    private val portRowHeight get() = dpF(36f)

    private var draggedNodeId: String? = null
    private var touchDownX = 0f
    private var touchDownY = 0f
    private var isDraggingNode = false

    private var isWiring = false
    private var wiringFromNodeId: String? = null
    private var wiringFromPortId: String? = null
    private var wiringStartX = 0f
    private var wiringStartY = 0f
    private var wiringCurrentX = 0f
    private var wiringCurrentY = 0f

    private val paintNodeBg = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#F8161B22".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.FILL }
    private val paintNodeHeader = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#21262D".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.FILL }
    private val paintBorder = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#30363D".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeWidth = dpF(1.5f) }
    private val paintTextTitle = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = Color.WHITE; textSize = dpF(11f); typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD }
    private val paintTextSub = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#8B949E".toColorInt(); textSize = dpF(9f) }
    private val paintEdge = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#58A6FF".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeWidth = dpF(2.5f); strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND }
    private val paintActiveWiring = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#00F5D4".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeWidth = dpF(3f); strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND }
    private val paintPortIn = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#38BDF8".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.FILL }
    private val paintPortOut = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = "#00F5D4".toColorInt(); style = Paint.Style.FILL }

    private val scaleDetector = ScaleGestureDetector(context, object : ScaleGestureDetector.SimpleOnScaleGestureListener() {
        override fun onScale(detector: ScaleGestureDetector): Boolean {
            val prevScale = scaleFactor
            scaleFactor = (scaleFactor * detector.scaleFactor).coerceIn(0.35f, 2.5f)
            val factor = scaleFactor / prevScale
            panX = detector.focusX - (detector.focusX - panX) * factor
            panY = detector.focusY - (detector.focusY - panY) * factor
            updateMatrix()
            invalidate()
            return true
        }
    })

    init { updateMatrix() }

    fun setGraphScenario(newScenario: GraphMacroScenario) {
        scenario = newScenario
        invalidate()
    }

    private fun updateMatrix() {
        canvasMatrix.reset()
        canvasMatrix.postScale(scaleFactor, scaleFactor)
        canvasMatrix.postTranslate(panX, panY)
        canvasMatrix.invert(inverseMatrix)
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        val sc = scenario ?: return
        canvas.save()
        canvas.concat(canvasMatrix)

        sc.edges.forEach { edge ->
            val from = sc.nodes[edge.fromNodeId] ?: return@forEach
            val to = sc.nodes[edge.toNodeId] ?: return@forEach
            val p1 = getPortPos(from, edge.fromPortId, true)
            val p2 = getPortPos(to, edge.toPortId, false)
            drawCurve(canvas, p1, p2, paintEdge)
        }

        if (isWiring) {
            drawCurve(canvas, PointF(wiringStartX, wiringStartY), PointF(wiringCurrentX, wiringCurrentY), paintActiveWiring)
        }

        sc.nodes.values.forEach { node ->
            val h = calculateHeight(node)
            val rect = RectF(node.canvasX, node.canvasY, node.canvasX + nodeWidth, node.canvasY + h)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(rect, dpF(10f), dpF(10f), paintNodeBg)
            
            val headRect = RectF(node.canvasX, node.canvasY, node.canvasX + nodeWidth, node.canvasY + headerHeight)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(headRect, dpF(10f), dpF(10f), paintNodeHeader)
            canvas.drawText(node.title, node.canvasX + dpF(8f), node.canvasY + headerHeight * 0.65f, paintTextTitle)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(rect, dpF(10f), dpF(10f), paintBorder)

            val inY = node.canvasY + headerHeight
            canvas.drawCircle(node.canvasX, inY, portRadius, paintPortIn)
            
            var py = node.canvasY + headerHeight + dpF(22f)
            node.triggers.forEach {
                canvas.drawText(it.name, node.canvasX + dpF(8f), py, paintTextSub)
                canvas.drawCircle(node.canvasX + nodeWidth, py - dpF(4f), portRadius, paintPortOut)
                py += portRowHeight
            }
            node.standardPorts.forEach {
                canvas.drawText(it, node.canvasX + dpF(8f), py, paintTextSub)
                canvas.drawCircle(node.canvasX + nodeWidth, py - dpF(4f), portRadius, paintPortOut)
                py += portRowHeight
            }
        }
        canvas.restore()
    }

    private fun calculateHeight(node: ScenarioNode): Float {
        return headerHeight + (node.triggers.size + node.standardPorts.size) * portRowHeight + dpF(16f)
    }

    private fun getPortPos(node: ScenarioNode, portId: String, isOut: Boolean): PointF {
        if (!isOut) return PointF(node.canvasX, node.canvasY + headerHeight)
        val idxTrig = node.triggers.indexOfFirst { it.targetPortId == portId }
        val py = if (idxTrig != -1) {
            node.canvasY + headerHeight + dpF(22f) + (idxTrig * portRowHeight) - dpF(4f)
        } else {
            val idxStd = node.standardPorts.indexOf(portId).coerceAtLeast(0)
            node.canvasY + headerHeight + dpF(22f) + (node.triggers.size * portRowHeight) + (idxStd * portRowHeight) - dpF(4f)
        }
        return PointF(node.canvasX + nodeWidth, py)
    }

    private fun drawCurve(canvas: Canvas, p1: PointF, p2: PointF, paint: Paint) {
        val path = Path()
        path.moveTo(p1.x, p1.y)
        val cp1 = p1.x + (p2.x - p1.x) / 2f
        path.cubicTo(cp1, p1.y, cp1, p2.y, p2.x, p2.y)
        canvas.drawPath(path, paint)
    }

    override fun onTouchEvent(event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
        scaleDetector.onTouchEvent(event)
        val pts = floatArrayOf(event.x, event.y)
        inverseMatrix.mapPoints(pts)
        val cx = pts[0]
        val cy = pts[1]

        val sc = scenario ?: return false

        when (event.actionMasked) {
            MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                touchDownX = cx
                touchDownY = cy
                isDraggingNode = false

                for (node in sc.nodes.values) {
                    var py = node.canvasY + headerHeight + dpF(22f)
                    val allOutPorts = node.triggers.map { it.targetPortId } + node.standardPorts
                    for (portId in allOutPorts) {
                        val portPos = getPortPos(node, portId, true)
                        if (hypot((cx - portPos.x).toDouble(), (cy - portPos.y).toDouble()) <= (portRadius * 2.5f)) {
                            isWiring = true
                            wiringFromNodeId = node.id
                            wiringFromPortId = portId
                            wiringStartX = portPos.x
                            wiringStartY = portPos.y
                            wiringCurrentX = cx
                            wiringCurrentY = cy
                            invalidate()
                            return true
                        }
                        py += portRowHeight
                    }
                }

                val touchedNode = sc.nodes.values.reversed().firstOrNull { 
                    cx in it.canvasX..(it.canvasX + nodeWidth) && cy in it.canvasY..(it.canvasY + calculateHeight(it))
                }
                if (touchedNode != null) {
                    draggedNodeId = touchedNode.id
                    return true
                }
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                if (isWiring) {
                    wiringCurrentX = cx
                    wiringCurrentY = cy
                    invalidate()
                    return true
                }
                draggedNodeId?.let { id ->
                    val dist = hypot((cx - touchDownX).toDouble(), (cy - touchDownY).toDouble())
                    if (dist > dpF(4f)) {
                        isDraggingNode = true
                    }
                    if (isDraggingNode) {
                        val nodes = sc.nodes.toMutableMap()
                        val n = nodes[id] ?: return@let
                        nodes[id] = n.copy(canvasX = cx - nodeWidth / 2, canvasY = cy - headerHeight / 2)
                        scenario = sc.copy(nodes = nodes)
                        invalidate()
                    }
                    return true
                }
            }
            MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                if (isWiring) {
                    val fromNode = wiringFromNodeId
                    val fromPort = wiringFromPortId
                    if (fromNode != null && fromPort != null) {
                        val targetNode = sc.nodes.values.firstOrNull { node ->
                            node.id != fromNode &&
                            hypot((cx - node.canvasX).toDouble(), (cy - (node.canvasY + headerHeight)).toDouble()) <= (portRadius * 3.5f)
                        }
                        if (targetNode != null) {
                            val newEdge = ScenarioEdge(
                                id = "edge_${UUID.randomUUID().toString().take(6)}",
                                fromNodeId = fromNode,
                                fromPortId = fromPort,
                                toNodeId = targetNode.id,
                                toPortId = "in_entry"
                            )
                            val mutableEdges = sc.edges.toMutableList()
                            mutableEdges.removeAll { it.fromNodeId == fromNode && it.fromPortId == fromPort }
                            mutableEdges.add(newEdge)
                            scenario = sc.copy(edges = mutableEdges)
                            onScenarioModified?.invoke()
                        }
                    }
                    isWiring = false
                    wiringFromNodeId = null
                    wiringFromPortId = null
                    invalidate()
                    return true
                }
                draggedNodeId?.let { id ->
                    if (!isDraggingNode) {
                        val n = sc.nodes[id]
                        if (n != null && cy <= n.canvasY + headerHeight) {
                            onNodeSelected?.invoke(n)
                        }
                    } else {
                        onScenarioModified?.invoke()
                    }
                    draggedNodeId = null
                    isDraggingNode = false
                    return true
                }
            }
        }
        return true
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.graph

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
import android.view.ViewGroup.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.graphics.toColorInt
import com.example.autotap.core.math.LinearToGraphMigrator
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.ActionType
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.EvaluationPolicy
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.GraphMacroScenario
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.NodeActionSpec
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.graph.ScenarioNode
import com.example.autotap.domain.repository.IScenarioRepository
import com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.OverlayWindowManager

@SuppressLint("SetTextI18n")
class GraphEditorOverlay(
    private val context: Context,
    private val overlayWindowManager: OverlayWindowManager,
    private val scenarioRepository: IScenarioRepository
) {
    private var overlayContainerView: FrameLayout? = null
    private var canvasView: GraphCanvasView? = null
    private var inspectorCard: LinearLayout? = null
    private var currentScenario: GraphMacroScenario? = null

    var onStartGraphRequested: ((GraphMacroScenario) -> Unit)? = null

    private val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
    private fun dp(v: Int): Int = (v * dm.density).toInt()
    private fun dpF(v: Float): Float = v * dm.density

    fun show(scenarioName: String) {
        if (overlayContainerView != null) return
        val graph = scenarioRepository.loadGraphScenario(scenarioName)
            ?: scenarioRepository.loadScenario(scenarioName)?.let { linear ->
                val converted = LinearToGraphMigrator.linearToGraph(linear)
                scenarioRepository.saveGraphScenario(converted)
                converted
            } ?: return

        currentScenario = graph

        val root = FrameLayout(context).apply { setBackgroundColor("#0D1117".toColorInt()) }
        overlayContainerView = root

        val canvas = GraphCanvasView(context).apply {
            setGraphScenario(graph)
            onNodeSelected = { showNodeInspector(it) }
            onScenarioModified = { this.scenario?.let { currentScenario = it } }
        }
        canvasView = canvas
        root.addView(canvas, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(MATCH_PARENT, MATCH_PARENT))

        val topBar = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            background = GradientDrawable(GradientDrawable.Orientation.TOP_BOTTOM, intArrayOf("#F8161B22".toColorInt(), "#00000000".toColorInt()))
            setPadding(dp(16), dp(32), dp(16), dp(16))
        }
        
        val tvTitle = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "ГРАФ: ${graph.name}"
            setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            textSize = 12f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, WRAP_CONTENT, 1f)
        }
        topBar.addView(tvTitle)

        val btnAddNode = Button(context).apply {
            text = "+ ФАЗА"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#1F6FEB".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(8f) }
            setPadding(dp(10), 0, dp(10), 0)
            setOnClickListener { addNewNode() }
        }
        topBar.addView(btnAddNode, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(WRAP_CONTENT, dp(36)).apply { marginEnd = dp(6) })

        val btnPlay = Button(context).apply {
            text = "▶ ПУСК"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#10B981".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(8f) }
            setPadding(dp(10), 0, dp(10), 0)
            setOnClickListener { 
                currentScenario?.let { onStartGraphRequested?.invoke(it) }
                dismiss() 
            }
        }
        topBar.addView(btnPlay, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(WRAP_CONTENT, dp(36)).apply { marginEnd = dp(6) })

        val btnSave = Button(context).apply {
            text = "СОХРАНИТЬ"
            setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#21262D".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(8f); setStroke(dp(1), "#30363D".toColorInt()) }
            setPadding(dp(10), 0, dp(10), 0)
            setOnClickListener { 
                currentScenario?.let { scenarioRepository.saveGraphScenario(it) }
                Toast.makeText(context, "Граф сохранен", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        topBar.addView(btnSave, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(WRAP_CONTENT, dp(36)).apply { marginEnd = dp(6) })

        val btnClose = Button(context).apply {
            text = "ЗАКРЫТЬ"
            setTextColor("#F04438".toColorInt())
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#21262D".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(8f) }
            setPadding(dp(10), 0, dp(10), 0)
            setOnClickListener { dismiss() }
        }
        topBar.addView(btnClose, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(WRAP_CONTENT, dp(36)))

        root.addView(topBar, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(MATCH_PARENT, WRAP_CONTENT, Gravity.TOP))
        overlayWindowManager.addViewSafe(root, overlayWindowManager.createLayoutParams(width = MATCH_PARENT, height = MATCH_PARENT))
    }

    private fun addNewNode() {
        val sc = currentScenario ?: return
        var nextNum = sc.nodes.size + 1
        while (sc.nodes.containsKey("node_$nextNum")) {
            nextNum++
        }
        val newNodeId = "node_$nextNum"
        val newNode = ScenarioNode(
            id = newNodeId,
            title = "Фаза #$nextNum",
            canvasX = 140f + ((nextNum % 5) * 40f),
            canvasY = 220f + ((nextNum % 5) * 30f),
            entryActions = listOf(NodeActionSpec("act_$nextNum", ActionType.CLICK, 500f, 1000f)),
            standardPorts = listOf("out_default")
        )
        val updatedNodes = sc.nodes.toMutableMap()
        updatedNodes[newNodeId] = newNode
        val updatedGraph = sc.copy(nodes = updatedNodes)
        currentScenario = updatedGraph
        canvasView?.setGraphScenario(updatedGraph)
        Toast.makeText(context, "Добавлена $newNodeId", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
    }

    private fun showNodeInspector(node: ScenarioNode) {
        inspectorCard?.let { overlayContainerView?.removeView(it) }
        
        val card = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            val p = dp(12)
            setPadding(p, p, p, p)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { 
                setColor("#F8161B22".toColorInt())
                setStroke(dp(1), "#00F5D4".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(16f)
            }
            elevation = dpF(24f)
        }
        inspectorCard = card

        val title = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "ФАЗА: ${node.title}"
            setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            textSize = 10.5f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(8))
        }
        card.addView(title)

        val desc = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "ID: ${node.id}\nТриггеров: ${node.triggers.size}\nДействий: ${node.entryActions.size}\nТаймаут: ${node.timeoutSeconds}с"
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            textSize = 9f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(12))
        }
        card.addView(desc)

        val btnRow1 = LinearLayout(context).apply { orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL }

        val btnEntry = Button(context).apply {
            text = "В СТАРТ"
            textSize = 7.5f
            setTextColor(Color.BLACK)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(4f) }
            setOnClickListener { 
                currentScenario = currentScenario?.copy(entryNodeId = node.id)
                currentScenario?.let { canvasView?.setGraphScenario(it) }
                overlayContainerView?.removeView(card)
                Toast.makeText(context, "Сделана стартовой", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        btnRow1.addView(btnEntry, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(30), 1f).apply { marginEnd = dp(4) })

        val btnClearEdges = Button(context).apply {
            text = "ОЧИСТИТЬ СВЯЗИ"
            textSize = 7.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#21262D".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(4f); setStroke(dp(1), "#30363D".toColorInt()) }
            setOnClickListener {
                val sc = currentScenario ?: return@setOnClickListener
                val edges = sc.edges.filterNot { it.fromNodeId == node.id || it.toNodeId == node.id }
                val updated = sc.copy(edges = edges)
                currentScenario = updated
                canvasView?.setGraphScenario(updated)
                overlayContainerView?.removeView(card)
                Toast.makeText(context, "Связи удалены", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        btnRow1.addView(btnClearEdges, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(30), 1.4f))
        card.addView(btnRow1)

        val btnRow2 = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, 0)
        }

        val btnDeleteNode = Button(context).apply {
            text = "УДАЛИТЬ ФАЗУ"
            textSize = 7.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#F04438".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(4f) }
            setOnClickListener {
                val sc = currentScenario ?: return@setOnClickListener
                if (sc.nodes.size <= 1) {
                    Toast.makeText(context, "Нельзя удалить единственную ноду", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    return@setOnClickListener
                }
                val nodes = sc.nodes.toMutableMap()
                nodes.remove(node.id)
                val edges = sc.edges.filterNot { it.fromNodeId == node.id || it.toNodeId == node.id }
                val newEntry = if (sc.entryNodeId == node.id) nodes.keys.first() else sc.entryNodeId
                val updated = sc.copy(nodes = nodes, edges = edges, entryNodeId = newEntry)
                currentScenario = updated
                canvasView?.setGraphScenario(updated)
                overlayContainerView?.removeView(card)
                Toast.makeText(context, "Фаза удалена", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
        btnRow2.addView(btnDeleteNode, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(30), 1.2f).apply { marginEnd = dp(4) })

        val btnCloseInspector = Button(context).apply { 
            text = "ЗАКРЫТЬ"
            textSize = 7.5f
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply { setColor("#21262D".toColorInt()); cornerRadius = dpF(4f) }
            setOnClickListener { overlayContainerView?.removeView(card) } 
        }
        btnRow2.addView(btnCloseInspector, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(30), 1f))
        card.addView(btnRow2)

        val lp = FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dp(220), WRAP_CONTENT, Gravity.END or Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL).apply { rightMargin = dp(24) }
        overlayContainerView?.addView(card, lp)
    }

    fun dismiss() {
        overlayContainerView?.let { overlayWindowManager.removeViewSafe(it) }
        overlayContainerView = null
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.graph

import android.content.Context
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.DashPathEffect
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.view.View
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.ActionType
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.MacroAction
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.hypot
import kotlin.math.sin

class GraphOverlayView(context: Context) : View(context) {

    private val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
    private fun dpF(v: Float) = v * dm.density

    private val swipeVectorPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = dpF(2.5f)
        color = Color.parseColor("#58A6FF")
        pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(dpF(7f), dpF(4f)), 0f)
    }

    private val swipeArrowHeadPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = Color.parseColor("#58A6FF")
    }

    private val matchPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = dpF(2f)
        color = Color.parseColor("#10B981")
        pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(dpF(6f), dpF(4f)), 0f)
    }

    private val timeoutPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        strokeWidth = dpF(2f)
        color = Color.parseColor("#F97316")
        pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(dpF(6f), dpF(4f)), 0f)
    }

    private val arrowFillMatch = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = Color.parseColor("#10B981")
    }

    private val arrowFillTimeout = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        style = Paint.Style.FILL
        color = Color.parseColor("#F97316")
    }

    private val reusableCurvePath = Path()
    private val reusableHeadPath = Path()

    @Volatile
    private var actionsSnapshot: List<MacroAction> = emptyList()

    init {
        setWillNotDraw(false)
        setLayerType(LAYER_TYPE_HARDWARE, null)
    }

    fun submitActions(actions: List<MacroAction>) {
        actionsSnapshot = ArrayList(actions)
        invalidate()
    }

    override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
        super.onDraw(canvas)
        val actions = actionsSnapshot
        if (actions.isEmpty()) return

        for (action in actions) {
            if (action.type == ActionType.SWIPE && action.endX != null && action.endY != null) {
                drawDirectArrow(canvas, action.posX, action.posY, action.endX, action.endY, swipeVectorPaint, swipeArrowHeadPaint)
            }
        }

        if (actions.size < 2) return
        for (action in actions) {
            val x1 = action.posX
            val y1 = action.posY

            val matchTargetId = action.jumpToStepOnMatch
            if (matchTargetId != null && matchTargetId in 1..actions.size && matchTargetId != action.id) {
                val target = actions[matchTargetId - 1]
                drawCurvedArrow(canvas, x1, y1, target.posX, target.posY, matchPaint, arrowFillMatch, dpF(20f))
            }

            val timeoutTargetId = action.jumpToStepOnTimeout
            if (timeoutTargetId != null && timeoutTargetId in 1..actions.size && timeoutTargetId != action.id) {
                val target = actions[timeoutTargetId - 1]
                drawCurvedArrow(canvas, x1, y1, target.posX, target.posY, timeoutPaint, arrowFillTimeout, -dpF(20f))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun drawDirectArrow(
        canvas: Canvas,
        x1: Float, y1: Float, x2: Float, y2: Float,
        linePaint: Paint, arrowPaint: Paint
    ) {
        val dx = x2 - x1
        val dy = y2 - y1
        val dist = hypot(dx.toDouble(), dy.toDouble()).toFloat()
        if (dist < dpF(4f)) return

        canvas.drawLine(x1, y1, x2, y2, linePaint)

        val angle = atan2(dy.toDouble(), dx.toDouble())
        val arrowHeadLen = dpF(10f)
        val arrowAngle = Math.PI / 6.0

        val p1X = (x2 - arrowHeadLen * cos(angle - arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p1Y = (y2 - arrowHeadLen * sin(angle - arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p2X = (x2 - arrowHeadLen * cos(angle + arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p2Y = (y2 - arrowHeadLen * sin(angle + arrowAngle)).toFloat()

        reusableHeadPath.rewind()
        reusableHeadPath.moveTo(x2, y2)
        reusableHeadPath.lineTo(p1X, p1Y)
        reusableHeadPath.lineTo(p2X, p2Y)
        reusableHeadPath.close()
        canvas.drawPath(reusableHeadPath, arrowPaint)
    }

    private fun drawCurvedArrow(
        canvas: Canvas,
        x1: Float, y1: Float, x2: Float, y2: Float,
        linePaint: Paint, arrowPaint: Paint,
        curvature: Float
    ) {
        val dx = x2 - x1
        val dy = y2 - y1
        val dist = hypot(dx.toDouble(), dy.toDouble()).toFloat()
        if (dist < dpF(5f)) return

        val normX = -dy / dist
        val normY = dx / dist
        val midX = (x1 + x2) / 2f + normX * curvature
        val midY = (y1 + y2) / 2f + normY * curvature

        reusableCurvePath.rewind()
        reusableCurvePath.moveTo(x1, y1)
        reusableCurvePath.quadTo(midX, midY, x2, y2)
        canvas.drawPath(reusableCurvePath, linePaint)

        val angle = atan2((y2 - midY).toDouble(), (x2 - midX).toDouble())
        val arrowHeadLen = dpF(9f)
        val arrowAngle = Math.PI / 6.0

        val p1X = (x2 - arrowHeadLen * cos(angle - arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p1Y = (y2 - arrowHeadLen * sin(angle - arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p2X = (x2 - arrowHeadLen * cos(angle + arrowAngle)).toFloat()
        val p2Y = (y2 - arrowHeadLen * sin(angle + arrowAngle)).toFloat()

        reusableHeadPath.rewind()
        reusableHeadPath.moveTo(x2, y2)
        reusableHeadPath.lineTo(p1X, p1Y)
        reusableHeadPath.lineTo(p2X, p2Y)
        reusableHeadPath.close()
        canvas.drawPath(reusableHeadPath, arrowPaint)
    }
}

```

```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.joystick

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService
import android.accessibilityservice.GestureDescription
import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.DashPathEffect
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Path
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.TextView
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.graphics.toColorInt
import com.example.autotap.core.logger.AppLogger
import com.example.autotap.core.math.PathCompressionEngine
import com.example.autotap.domain.model.Point2D
import com.example.autotap.infrastructure.accessibility.AutoTapAccessibilityService
import com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.OverlayWindowManager
import com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.ui.VectorIconDrawer
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.atan2
import kotlin.math.cos
import kotlin.math.hypot
import kotlin.math.min
import kotlin.math.sin

@SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility", "SetTextI18n")
class JoystickOverlay(
    private val context: Context,
    private val overlayWindowManager: OverlayWindowManager,
    private val onPathRecorded: (List<Point2D>, Long) -> Unit
) {

    private var controlPadView: FrameLayout? = null
    private var targetReticleView: FrameLayout? = null

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
    private fun dp(v: Int): Int = (v * dm.density).toInt()
    private fun dpF(v: Float): Float = v * dm.density

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences = context.getSharedPreferences("autotap_joystick_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    private val padWidth get() = dp(160)
    private val padHeight get() = dp(210)
    private val knobRadiusLimit get() = dpF(46f)
    private val reticleSize get() = dp(110)

    private var isReticleLocked = true

    private var padParams: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null
    private var reticleParams: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null

    @Volatile
    private var isTouchingStick = false
    private var isRecording = false
    private var isLiveEnabled = true

    private var currentKnobX = 0f
    private var currentKnobY = 0f
    private var normalizedMagnitude = 0f

    private var smoothedTargetX = 0f
    private var smoothedTargetY = 0f
    private val filterAlpha = 0.65f

    private val recordedPoints = ArrayList<Point2D>()
    private var recordingStartTime = 0L

    private var activeChainedStroke: GestureDescription.StrokeDescription? = null
    private var lastDispatchedX = 0f
    private var lastDispatchedY = 0f
    private var isGestureInFlight = false

    private var tvTelemetry: TextView? = null
    private var btnRecord: Button? = null
    private var reticleCenterDot: View? = null
    private var btnReticleLockToggle: Button? = null

    fun show() {
        if (controlPadView != null) return
        AppLogger.log(context, "JOYSTICK", "Запуск игрового аналогового стика 360 (Continuous Chained FSM)")

        val screenW = dm.widthPixels
        val screenH = dm.heightPixels

        val savedPadX = prefs.getInt("pad_x", dp(20)).coerceIn(0, (screenW - padWidth).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val savedPadY = prefs.getInt("pad_y", screenH - padHeight - dp(70)).coerceIn(0, (screenH - padHeight).coerceAtLeast(0))

        val savedReticleX = prefs.getInt("reticle_x", screenW / 2 - reticleSize / 2).coerceIn(0, (screenW - reticleSize).coerceAtLeast(0))
        val savedReticleY = prefs.getInt("reticle_y", screenH / 2 - reticleSize / 2).coerceIn(0, (screenH - reticleSize).coerceAtLeast(0))

        val pParams = overlayWindowManager.createLayoutParams(
            width = padWidth,
            height = padHeight,
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
        ).apply {
            x = savedPadX
            y = savedPadY
        }
        padParams = pParams

        val rParams = overlayWindowManager.createLayoutParams(
            width = reticleSize,
            height = reticleSize,
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
        ).apply {
            x = savedReticleX
            y = savedReticleY
            flags = flags or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE
        }
        reticleParams = rParams

        buildTargetReticleOverlay()
        buildControlPadOverlay()

        overlayWindowManager.addViewSafe(targetReticleView, rParams)
        overlayWindowManager.addViewSafe(controlPadView, pParams)
    }

    private fun setReticleTouchable(touchable: Boolean) {
        isReticleLocked = !touchable
        val params = reticleParams ?: return
        if (touchable) {
            params.flags = params.flags and WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE.inv()
        } else {
            params.flags = params.flags or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE
        }
        overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(targetReticleView, params)
        btnReticleLockToggle?.text = if (isReticleLocked) "МИШЕНЬ: 🔒" else "МИШЕНЬ: ✋"
        btnReticleLockToggle?.setTextColor(if (isReticleLocked) Color.BLACK else Color.WHITE)
        btnReticleLockToggle?.background = GradientDrawable().apply {
            setColor(if (isReticleLocked) "#00F5D4".toColorInt() else "#F04438".toColorInt())
            cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
        }
        targetReticleView?.invalidate()
    }

    private fun savePositions() {
        val p = padParams
        val r = reticleParams
        if (p != null && r != null) {
            prefs.edit()
                .putInt("pad_x", p.x)
                .putInt("pad_y", p.y)
                .putInt("reticle_x", r.x)
                .putInt("reticle_y", r.y)
                .apply()
        }
    }

    private fun buildTargetReticleOverlay() {
        val root = object : FrameLayout(context) {
            private val ringPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(2.5f)
                color = "#FFB703".toColorInt()
            }
            private val crosshairPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(1.2f)
                color = "#80FFB703".toColorInt()
                pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(dpF(4f), dpF(3f)), 0f)
            }
            private val fillPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
                color = "#15FFB703".toColorInt()
            }
            private val editBorderPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(2f)
                color = "#F04438".toColorInt()
                pathEffect = DashPathEffect(floatArrayOf(dpF(6f), dpF(4f)), 0f)
            }

            override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
                super.onDraw(canvas)
                val cx = width / 2f
                val cy = height / 2f
                val radius = (width / 2f) - dpF(4f)

                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, fillPaint)
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, ringPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(cx - radius, cy, cx + radius, cy, crosshairPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(cx, cy - radius, cx, cy + radius, crosshairPaint)

                if (!isReticleLocked) {
                    canvas.drawRoundRect(RectF(dpF(2f), dpF(2f), width - dpF(2f), height - dpF(2f)), dpF(8f), dpF(8f), editBorderPaint)
                }
            }
        }
        root.setWillNotDraw(false)

        val badge = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "D-PAD МИШЕНЬ"
            setTextColor("#FFB703".toColorInt())
            textSize = 7.5f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            includeFontPadding = false
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#EE0D1117".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(3f)
                setStroke(dp(1), "#FFB703".toColorInt())
            }
            setPadding(dp(4), dp(1), dp(4), dp(1))
        }
        root.addView(badge, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, Gravity.TOP or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL))

        val activeDot = View(context).apply {
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                shape = GradientDrawable.OVAL
                setColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
                setStroke(dp(1), Color.WHITE)
            }
        }
        val dotSz = dp(16)
        reticleCenterDot = activeDot
        root.addView(activeDot, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(dotSz, dotSz, Gravity.CENTER))

        root.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initX = 0
            private var initY = 0
            private var touchX = 0f
            private var touchY = 0f

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                if (isReticleLocked) return false
                val params = reticleParams ?: return false
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initX = params.x
                        initY = params.y
                        touchX = event.rawX
                        touchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val screenW = dm.widthPixels
                        val screenH = dm.heightPixels
                        params.x = (initX + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()).coerceIn(0, screenW - reticleSize)
                        params.y = (initY + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()).coerceIn(0, screenH - reticleSize)
                        overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(targetReticleView, params)
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                        savePositions()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        targetReticleView = root
    }

    private fun buildControlPadOverlay() {
        val root = FrameLayout(context)

        val card = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            background = GradientDrawable(
                GradientDrawable.Orientation.TOP_BOTTOM,
                intArrayOf("#F8161B22".toColorInt(), "#F80D1117".toColorInt())
            ).apply {
                cornerRadius = dpF(16f)
                setStroke(dp(1), "#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            }
            val p = dp(6)
            setPadding(p, p, p, p)
            elevation = dpF(18f)
        }

        val headerBar = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#21262D".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(8f)
            }
            setPadding(dp(6), dp(3), dp(6), dp(3))
        }

        val dragHandle = createIconButton(VectorIconDrawer.IconType.DRAG_HANDLE, "#161B22", "#58A6FF", dp(24)) {}
        headerBar.addView(dragHandle)

        val tvTitle = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "СТИК 360"
            setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            textSize = 9.5f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            includeFontPadding = false
            setPadding(dp(6), 0, 0, 0)
        }
        headerBar.addView(tvTitle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f))

        val btnClose = createIconButton(VectorIconDrawer.IconType.CLOSE, "#21262D", "#F04438", dp(24)) {
            dismiss()
        }
        headerBar.addView(btnClose)
        card.addView(headerBar)

        val stickPadFrame = FrameLayout(context).apply {
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, dp(115)).apply {
                val m = dp(4)
                setMargins(0, m, 0, m)
            }
        }

        val baseCircle = object : View(context) {
            private val bgPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
                color = "#E00D1117".toColorInt()
            }
            private val ringPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(1.5f)
                color = "#00F5D4".toColorInt()
            }
            private val crossPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(1f)
                color = "#3000F5D4".toColorInt()
            }

            override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
                super.onDraw(canvas)
                val cx = width / 2f
                val cy = height / 2f
                val radius = (minOf(width, height) / 2f) - dpF(3f)

                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, bgPaint)
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, ringPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(cx - radius, cy, cx + radius, cy, crossPaint)
                canvas.drawLine(cx, cy - radius, cx, cy + radius, crossPaint)
            }
        }
        stickPadFrame.addView(baseCircle, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT))

        val knob = object : View(context) {
            private val fillPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
                color = "#1F6FEB".toColorInt()
            }
            private val strokePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                strokeWidth = dpF(2f)
                color = "#00F5D4".toColorInt()
            }
            private val centerDotPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
                style = Paint.Style.FILL
                color = Color.WHITE
            }

            override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
                super.onDraw(canvas)
                val cx = width / 2f
                val cy = height / 2f
                val radius = (minOf(width, height) / 2f) - dpF(2f)

                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, fillPaint)
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, radius, strokePaint)
                canvas.drawCircle(cx, cy, dpF(3.5f), centerDotPaint)
            }
        }
        val knobSize = dp(44)
        stickPadFrame.addView(knob, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(knobSize, knobSize, Gravity.CENTER))
        card.addView(stickPadFrame)

        val tvTelem = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "Угол: 0° | Спринт: 0%"
            setTextColor("#8B949E".toColorInt())
            textSize = 8.5f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            includeFontPadding = false
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(3))
        }
        tvTelemetry = tvTelem
        card.addView(tvTelem)

        val actionRow = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
        }

        val btnRec = Button(context).apply {
            text = "ЗАПИСЬ"
            textSize = 8.5f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            includeFontPadding = false
            setTextColor(Color.WHITE)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#1F6FEB".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
            }
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dp(4), dp(4), dp(4), dp(4))
            setOnClickListener { toggleRecording() }
        }
        btnRecord = btnRec
        actionRow.addView(btnRec, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(28), 1.1f).apply { marginEnd = dp(2) })

        val btnLive = Button(context).apply {
            text = "LIVE: ON"
            textSize = 7.5f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            includeFontPadding = false
            setTextColor(Color.BLACK)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
            }
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dp(2), dp(4), dp(2), dp(4))
            setOnClickListener {
                isLiveEnabled = !isLiveEnabled
                text = if (isLiveEnabled) "LIVE: ON" else "LIVE: OFF"
                setTextColor(if (isLiveEnabled) Color.BLACK else Color.WHITE)
                background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                    setColor(if (isLiveEnabled) "#00F5D4".toColorInt() else "#21262D".toColorInt())
                    cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
                }
            }
        }
        actionRow.addView(btnLive, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(28), 0.95f).apply { marginEnd = dp(2) })

        val btnLockToggle = Button(context).apply {
            text = "МИШЕНЬ: 🔒"
            textSize = 7.5f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            includeFontPadding = false
            setTextColor(Color.BLACK)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
            }
            minHeight = 0
            minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dp(2), dp(4), dp(2), dp(4))
            setOnClickListener {
                setReticleTouchable(isReticleLocked)
            }
        }
        btnReticleLockToggle = btnLockToggle
        actionRow.addView(btnLockToggle, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, dp(28), 1.05f))
        card.addView(actionRow)

        root.addView(card, FrameLayout.LayoutParams(FrameLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, FrameLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT))

        headerBar.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            private var initX = 0
            private var initY = 0
            private var touchX = 0f
            private var touchY = 0f

            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                val params = padParams ?: return false
                when (event.action) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        initX = params.x
                        initY = params.y
                        touchX = event.rawX
                        touchY = event.rawY
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        val screenW = dm.widthPixels
                        val screenH = dm.heightPixels
                        params.x = (initX + (event.rawX - touchX).toInt()).coerceIn(0, screenW - padWidth)
                        params.y = (initY + (event.rawY - touchY).toInt()).coerceIn(0, screenH - padHeight)
                        overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(controlPadView, params)
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                        savePositions()
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        stickPadFrame.setOnTouchListener(object : View.OnTouchListener {
            override fun onTouch(v: View, event: MotionEvent): Boolean {
                val padCenterX = v.width / 2f
                val padCenterY = v.height / 2f
                val deltaX = event.x - padCenterX
                val deltaY = event.y - padCenterY

                when (event.actionMasked) {
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                        isTouchingStick = true
                        startInitialChainedGesture()
                        updateStickPosition(deltaX, deltaY, knob)
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                        updateStickPosition(deltaX, deltaY, knob)
                        return true
                    }
                    MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                        isTouchingStick = false
                        completeAndReleaseChainedGesture()
                        currentKnobX = 0f
                        currentKnobY = 0f
                        normalizedMagnitude = 0f
                        knob.animate().translationX(0f).translationY(0f).setDuration(80).start()
                        reticleCenterDot?.animate()?.translationX(0f)?.translationY(0f)?.setDuration(80)?.start()
                        tvTelemetry?.text = "Угол: 0° | Спринт: 0%"
                        return true
                    }
                }
                return false
            }
        })

        controlPadView = root
    }

    private fun updateStickPosition(dx: Float, dy: Float, knobView: View) {
        val dist = hypot(dx.toDouble(), dy.toDouble()).toFloat()
        val angle = atan2(dy.toDouble(), dx.toDouble())
        val clampedDist = min(dist, knobRadiusLimit)

        val kx = (clampedDist * cos(angle)).toFloat()
        val ky = (clampedDist * sin(angle)).toFloat()
        knobView.translationX = kx
        knobView.translationY = ky

        currentKnobX = kx
        currentKnobY = ky

        val rawMag = (clampedDist / knobRadiusLimit).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        normalizedMagnitude = when {
            rawMag < 0.12f -> 0f
            rawMag > 0.75f -> 1.0f
            else -> (rawMag - 0.12f) / (0.75f - 0.12f)
        }

        var deg = Math.toDegrees(angle)
        if (deg < 0) deg += 360.0
        val powerPct = (normalizedMagnitude * 100).toInt()
        val zoneTag = if (normalizedMagnitude >= 1.0f) " [СПРИНТ]" else ""
        tvTelemetry?.text = String.format(Locale.US, "Угол: %d° | %d%%%s", deg.toInt(), powerPct, zoneTag)

        val reticleRadius = (reticleSize / 2f) - dpF(12f)
        val rx = (normalizedMagnitude * reticleRadius * cos(angle)).toFloat()
        val ry = (normalizedMagnitude * reticleRadius * sin(angle)).toFloat()
        reticleCenterDot?.translationX = rx
        reticleCenterDot?.translationY = ry

        val rParams = reticleParams ?: return
        val reticleCenterX = rParams.x + reticleSize / 2f
        val reticleCenterY = rParams.y + reticleSize / 2f
        val targetGameX = (reticleCenterX + rx).coerceIn(0f, (dm.widthPixels - 1).toFloat())
        val targetGameY = (reticleCenterY + ry).coerceIn(0f, (dm.heightPixels - 1).toFloat())

        smoothedTargetX = if (smoothedTargetX == 0f) targetGameX else (smoothedTargetX * (1 - filterAlpha) + targetGameX * filterAlpha)
        smoothedTargetY = if (smoothedTargetY == 0f) targetGameY else (smoothedTargetY * (1 - filterAlpha) + targetGameY * filterAlpha)

        if (isRecording && normalizedMagnitude > 0f) {
            val last = recordedPoints.lastOrNull()
            if (last == null || hypot((targetGameX - last.x).toDouble(), (targetGameY - last.y).toDouble()) > 3.0) {
                recordedPoints.add(Point2D(targetGameX, targetGameY))
            }
        }
    }

    private fun getCurrentTargetPoint(): Pair<Float, Float> {
        val rParams = reticleParams
        val reticleCenterX = (rParams?.x ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f
        val reticleCenterY = (rParams?.y ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f
        val reticleRadius = (reticleSize / 2f) - dpF(12f)

        val angle = atan2(currentKnobY.toDouble(), currentKnobX.toDouble())
        val rx = (normalizedMagnitude * reticleRadius * cos(angle)).toFloat()
        val ry = (normalizedMagnitude * reticleRadius * sin(angle)).toFloat()

        val rawX = (reticleCenterX + rx).coerceIn(0f, (dm.widthPixels - 1).toFloat())
        val rawY = (reticleCenterY + ry).coerceIn(0f, (dm.heightPixels - 1).toFloat())

        smoothedTargetX = if (smoothedTargetX == 0f) rawX else (smoothedTargetX * (1 - filterAlpha) + rawX * filterAlpha)
        smoothedTargetY = if (smoothedTargetY == 0f) rawY else (smoothedTargetY * (1 - filterAlpha) + rawY * filterAlpha)

        return Pair(smoothedTargetX, smoothedTargetY)
    }

    private fun startInitialChainedGesture() {
        if (!isLiveEnabled) return
        val service = AutoTapAccessibilityService.instance ?: return
        if (isGestureInFlight) return

        val (targetX, targetY) = getCurrentTargetPoint()
        val rParams = reticleParams
        val startFromX = if (lastDispatchedX > 0f) lastDispatchedX else ((rParams?.x ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f)
        val startFromY = if (lastDispatchedY > 0f) lastDispatchedY else ((rParams?.y ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f)

        lastDispatchedX = targetX
        lastDispatchedY = targetY
        isGestureInFlight = true

        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(startFromX, startFromY)
            lineTo(targetX, targetY)
        }

        val segmentDuration = 45L
        val stroke = if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0L, segmentDuration, true)
        } else {
            GestureDescription.StrokeDescription(path, 0L, segmentDuration)
        }
        activeChainedStroke = stroke

        val gesture = GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(stroke).build()
        try {
            service.dispatchGesture(gesture, object : AccessibilityService.GestureResultCallback() {
                override fun onCompleted(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    isGestureInFlight = false
                    onChainedSegmentCompleted()
                }

                override fun onCancelled(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    isGestureInFlight = false
                    activeChainedStroke = null
                    if (isTouchingStick) {
                        mainHandler.postDelayed({ if (isTouchingStick) startInitialChainedGesture() }, 30L)
                    }
                }
            }, mainHandler)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            isGestureInFlight = false
            activeChainedStroke = null
        }
    }

    private fun onChainedSegmentCompleted() {
        if (!isTouchingStick || !isLiveEnabled || Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
            completeAndReleaseChainedGesture()
            return
        }
        val service = AutoTapAccessibilityService.instance ?: return
        val (nextX, nextY) = getCurrentTargetPoint()

        val path = Path().apply {
            moveTo(lastDispatchedX, lastDispatchedY)
            lineTo(nextX, nextY)
        }

        lastDispatchedX = nextX
        lastDispatchedY = nextY
        isGestureInFlight = true

        val segmentDuration = 45L
        val nextStroke = try {
            activeChainedStroke?.continueStroke(path, 0L, segmentDuration, true)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }

        if (nextStroke == null) {
            isGestureInFlight = false
            activeChainedStroke = null
            startInitialChainedGesture()
            return
        }

        activeChainedStroke = nextStroke
        val gesture = GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(nextStroke).build()

        try {
            service.dispatchGesture(gesture, object : AccessibilityService.GestureResultCallback() {
                override fun onCompleted(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    isGestureInFlight = false
                    onChainedSegmentCompleted()
                }

                override fun onCancelled(gestureDescription: GestureDescription?) {
                    isGestureInFlight = false
                    activeChainedStroke = null
                    if (isTouchingStick) {
                        mainHandler.postDelayed({ if (isTouchingStick) startInitialChainedGesture() }, 30L)
                    }
                }
            }, mainHandler)
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            isGestureInFlight = false
            activeChainedStroke = null
        }
    }

    private fun completeAndReleaseChainedGesture() {
        val service = AutoTapAccessibilityService.instance ?: return
        val rParams = reticleParams
        val reticleCenterX = (rParams?.x ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f
        val reticleCenterY = (rParams?.y ?: 0) + reticleSize / 2f

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O && activeChainedStroke != null) {
            val path = Path().apply {
                moveTo(lastDispatchedX, lastDispatchedY)
                lineTo(lastDispatchedX, lastDispatchedY)
            }
            try {
                val finalStroke = activeChainedStroke?.continueStroke(path, 0L, 10L, false)
                if (finalStroke != null) {
                    service.dispatchGesture(GestureDescription.Builder().addStroke(finalStroke).build(), null, mainHandler)
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        activeChainedStroke = null
        isGestureInFlight = false
        lastDispatchedX = reticleCenterX
        lastDispatchedY = reticleCenterY
        smoothedTargetX = 0f
        smoothedTargetY = 0f
    }

    private fun toggleRecording() {
        if (!isRecording) {
            isRecording = true
            recordedPoints.clear()
            recordingStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
            btnRecord?.text = "СОХРАНИТЬ"
            btnRecord?.background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#F04438".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
            }
            Toast.makeText(context, "Запись движения стиком...", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } else {
            isRecording = false
            btnRecord?.text = "ЗАПИСЬ"
            btnRecord?.background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor("#1F6FEB".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
            }
            val duration = (System.currentTimeMillis() - recordingStartTime).coerceAtLeast(100L)
            if (recordedPoints.size >= 2) {
                val compressed = PathCompressionEngine.compressPath(recordedPoints.toList(), maxPoints = 80)
                onPathRecorded(compressed, duration)
                Toast.makeText(context, "Путь сохранен (${compressed.size} контрольных точек)!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                dismiss()
            } else {
                Toast.makeText(context, "Двигайте стик для записи траектории", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
            }
        }
    }

    fun dismiss() {
        isTouchingStick = false
        isRecording = false
        completeAndReleaseChainedGesture()
        savePositions()

        controlPadView?.let {
            overlayWindowManager.removeViewSafe(it)
            controlPadView = null
        }
        targetReticleView?.let {
            overlayWindowManager.removeViewSafe(it)
            targetReticleView = null
        }
    }

    private fun createIconButton(type: VectorIconDrawer.IconType, bgHex: String, iconHex: String, sizePx: Int, onClick: () -> Unit): View {
        return object : View(context) {
            private val bounds = RectF()
            override fun onDraw(canvas: Canvas) {
                super.onDraw(canvas)
                bounds.set(dpF(5f), dpF(5f), width - dpF(5f), height - dpF(5f))
                VectorIconDrawer.drawIcon(canvas, type, bounds, iconHex.toColorInt(), dpF(1.8f))
            }
        }.apply {
            background = GradientDrawable(
                GradientDrawable.Orientation.TOP_BOTTOM,
                intArrayOf(bgHex.toColorInt(), "#161B22".toColorInt())
            ).apply {
                cornerRadius = dpF(4f)
                setStroke(dp(1), iconHex.toColorInt())
            }
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(sizePx, sizePx)
            setOnClickListener { onClick() }
        }
    }
}

```

```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.lock

import android.annotation.SuppressLint
import android.content.Context
import android.content.SharedPreferences
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.drawable.ClipDrawable
import android.graphics.drawable.GradientDrawable
import android.graphics.drawable.LayerDrawable
import android.os.Build
import android.os.Handler
import android.os.Looper
import android.os.VibrationEffect
import android.os.Vibrator
import android.view.Gravity
import android.view.MotionEvent
import android.view.View
import android.view.WindowManager
import android.widget.Button
import android.widget.FrameLayout
import android.widget.LinearLayout
import android.widget.ProgressBar
import android.widget.SeekBar
import android.widget.TextView
import androidx.core.graphics.toColorInt
import com.example.autotap.core.logger.AppLogger
import com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.OverlayWindowManager
import java.util.Locale
import kotlin.math.hypot
import kotlin.random.Random

@SuppressLint("ClickableViewAccessibility", "SetTextI18n")
class ScreenLockOverlay(
    private val context: Context,
    private val overlayWindowManager: OverlayWindowManager
) {

    private var bgLockContainer: FrameLayout? = null
    private var uiLockContainer: LinearLayout? = null
    private var bgWindowParams: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null
    private var uiWindowParams: WindowManager.LayoutParams? = null

    private val mainHandler = Handler(Looper.getMainLooper())
    private val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
    private fun dp(v: Int): Int = (v * dm.density).toInt()
    private fun dpF(v: Float): Float = v * dm.density

    private var countdownRunnable: Runnable? = null
    private var pixelShiftRunnable: Runnable? = null
    private var holdStartTime = 0L
    private val totalHoldDurationMs = 3000L
    private var sessionStartTime = 0L

    private val prefs: SharedPreferences = context.getSharedPreferences("autotap_lock_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private var isBlackScreenMode: Boolean
        get() = prefs.getBoolean("is_black_screen", true)
        set(value) = prefs.edit().putBoolean("is_black_screen", value).apply()

    private var lockBrightness: Float
        get() = prefs.getFloat("lock_brightness", 0.01f)
        set(value) = prefs.edit().putFloat("lock_brightness", value).apply()

    var onLockDismissedListener: (() -> Unit)? = null

    fun isShowing(): Boolean = bgLockContainer != null

    fun setTemporarilyTransparent(transparent: Boolean) {
        mainHandler.post {
            bgLockContainer?.alpha = if (transparent) 0f else 1f
            uiLockContainer?.alpha = if (transparent) 0f else 1f
        }
    }

    fun setPassthroughEnabled(enabled: Boolean) {
        val bg = bgLockContainer ?: return
        val params = bgWindowParams ?: return
        mainHandler.post {
            if (enabled) {
                params.flags = params.flags or WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE
            } else {
                params.flags = params.flags and WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCHABLE.inv()
            }
            overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(bg, params)
        }
    }

    fun show() {
        if (isShowing()) return
        AppLogger.log(context, "LOCK", "Активация AFK защиты экрана (AMOLED Burn-in Guard)")
        sessionStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()

        val bg = FrameLayout(context).apply {
            setBackgroundColor(if (isBlackScreenMode) Color.BLACK else "#E6000000".toColorInt())
            setOnTouchListener { _, _ -> true }
        }
        bgLockContainer = bg

        val bgParams = overlayWindowManager.createLayoutParams(
            width = WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            height = WindowManager.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
            flags = WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN or
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_NO_LIMITS,
            gravity = Gravity.TOP or Gravity.START
        ).apply {
            screenBrightness = lockBrightness.coerceAtLeast(0.01f)
        }
        bgWindowParams = bgParams
        overlayWindowManager.addViewSafe(bg, bgParams)

        val anchorCard = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
            background = GradientDrawable(
                GradientDrawable.Orientation.TOP_BOTTOM,
                intArrayOf("#F0161B22".toColorInt(), "#F00D1117".toColorInt())
            ).apply {
                cornerRadius = dpF(20f)
                setStroke(dp(1), "#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            }
            val px = dp(16)
            val py = dp(10)
            setPadding(px, py, px, py)
            elevation = dpF(24f)
        }
        uiLockContainer = anchorCard

        val unlockTouchArea = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.VERTICAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT)
        }

        val tvStatus = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "🔒 УДЕРЖИВАЙТЕ ЗДЕСЬ 3 СЕК"
            setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            textSize = 10.5f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            includeFontPadding = false
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            setPadding(0, dp(4), 0, dp(4))
        }
        unlockTouchArea.addView(tvStatus)

        val tvUptime = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "СЕССИЯ: 00:00:00"
            setTextColor("#8B949E".toColorInt())
            textSize = 8.5f
            typeface = Typeface.MONOSPACE
            includeFontPadding = false
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER
            setPadding(0, 0, 0, dp(6))
        }
        unlockTouchArea.addView(tvUptime)

        val bgProgress = GradientDrawable().apply {
            setColor("#21262D".toColorInt())
            cornerRadius = dpF(3f)
        }
        val fillProgress = GradientDrawable().apply {
            setColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
            cornerRadius = dpF(3f)
        }
        val clipFill = ClipDrawable(fillProgress, Gravity.START, ClipDrawable.HORIZONTAL)
        val progressDrawable = LayerDrawable(arrayOf(bgProgress, clipFill)).apply {
            setId(0, android.R.id.background)
            setId(1, android.R.id.progress)
        }

        val pBar = ProgressBar(context, null, android.R.attr.progressBarStyleHorizontal).apply {
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(210), dp(5)).apply {
                bottomMargin = dp(8)
            }
            max = 100
            progress = 0
            this.progressDrawable = progressDrawable
        }
        unlockTouchArea.addView(pBar)
        anchorCard.addView(unlockTouchArea)

        val controlsRow = LinearLayout(context).apply {
            orientation = LinearLayout.HORIZONTAL
            gravity = Gravity.CENTER_VERTICAL
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(LinearLayout.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT)
        }

        val btnMode = Button(context).apply {
            text = if (isBlackScreenMode) "AMOLED" else "ТЕМНЫЙ"
            textSize = 8f
            typeface = Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD
            includeFontPadding = false
            setTextColor(Color.BLACK)
            background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                setColor(if (isBlackScreenMode) "#00F5D4".toColorInt() else "#58A6FF".toColorInt())
                cornerRadius = dpF(6f)
            }
            minHeight = 0; minimumHeight = 0
            setPadding(dp(8), dp(3), dp(8), dp(3))
            setOnClickListener {
                isBlackScreenMode = !isBlackScreenMode
                text = if (isBlackScreenMode) "AMOLED" else "ТЕМНЫЙ"
                bgLockContainer?.setBackgroundColor(if (isBlackScreenMode) Color.BLACK else "#E6000000".toColorInt())
                background = GradientDrawable().apply {
                    setColor(if (isBlackScreenMode) "#00F5D4".toColorInt() else "#58A6FF".toColorInt())
                    cornerRadius = dpF(6f)
                }
            }
        }
        controlsRow.addView(btnMode, LinearLayout.LayoutParams(dp(70), dp(28)))

        val tvSun = TextView(context).apply {
            text = "☀️"
            textSize = 12f
            setPadding(dp(8), 0, dp(2), 0)
        }
        controlsRow.addView(tvSun)

        val sbBrightness = SeekBar(context).apply {
            max = 100
            progress = (lockBrightness * 100).toInt().coerceIn(1, 100)
            layoutParams = LinearLayout.LayoutParams(0, LinearLayout.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT, 1f)
            setOnSeekBarChangeListener(object : SeekBar.OnSeekBarChangeListener {
                override fun onProgressChanged(seekBar: SeekBar?, prog: Int, fromUser: Boolean) {
                    if (fromUser) {
                        val newBr = prog.coerceAtLeast(1) / 100f
                        lockBrightness = newBr
                        val p = bgWindowParams ?: return
                        p.screenBrightness = newBr
                        bgLockContainer?.let { overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(it, p) }
                    }
                }
                override fun onStartTrackingTouch(seekBar: SeekBar?) {}
                override fun onStopTrackingTouch(seekBar: SeekBar?) {}
            })
        }
        controlsRow.addView(sbBrightness)
        anchorCard.addView(controlsRow)

        var touchDownX = 0f
        var touchDownY = 0f

        fun stopCountdown() {
            countdownRunnable?.let { mainHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
            countdownRunnable = null
            pBar.progress = 0
            tvStatus.text = "🔒 УДЕРЖИВАЙТЕ ЗДЕСЬ 3 СЕК"
            tvStatus.setTextColor("#00F5D4".toColorInt())
        }

        fun vibrate(ms: Long) {
            try {
                val vibrator = context.getSystemService(Vibrator::class.java)
                if (vibrator != null && vibrator.hasVibrator()) {
                    if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O) {
                        vibrator.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(ms, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
                    } else {
                        @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                        vibrator.vibrate(ms)
                    }
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
        }

        fun startCountdown() {
            holdStartTime = System.currentTimeMillis()
            vibrate(25L)

            countdownRunnable = object : Runnable {
                override fun run() {
                    val elapsed = System.currentTimeMillis() - holdStartTime
                    val remainingMs = (totalHoldDurationMs - elapsed).coerceAtLeast(0L)
                    val remainingSec = remainingMs / 1000.0
                    val pct = ((elapsed.toFloat() / totalHoldDurationMs.toFloat()) * 100).toInt().coerceIn(0, 100)

                    pBar.progress = pct

                    if (remainingMs <= 0) {
                        vibrate(60L)
                        dismiss()
                    } else {
                        tvStatus.text = String.format(Locale.US, "🔓 СНЯТИЕ: %.1f с", remainingSec)
                        tvStatus.setTextColor("#FFB703".toColorInt())
                        mainHandler.postDelayed(this, 40L)
                    }
                }
            }
            mainHandler.post(countdownRunnable as Runnable)
        }

        unlockTouchArea.setOnTouchListener { _, event ->
            when (event.action) {
                MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN -> {
                    touchDownX = event.rawX
                    touchDownY = event.rawY
                    startCountdown()
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_MOVE -> {
                    if (hypot((event.rawX - touchDownX).toDouble(), (event.rawY - touchDownY).toDouble()) > dp(50)) {
                        stopCountdown()
                    }
                    true
                }
                MotionEvent.ACTION_UP, MotionEvent.ACTION_CANCEL -> {
                    stopCountdown()
                    true
                }
                else -> false
            }
        }

        val uiParams = overlayWindowManager.createLayoutParams(
            width = dp(260),
            height = WindowManager.LayoutParams.WRAP_CONTENT,
            flags = WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_TOUCH_MODAL or
                    WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_LAYOUT_IN_SCREEN,
            gravity = Gravity.BOTTOM or Gravity.CENTER_HORIZONTAL
        ).apply {
            y = dp(60)
        }
        uiWindowParams = uiParams
        overlayWindowManager.addViewSafe(anchorCard, uiParams)

        pixelShiftRunnable = object : Runnable {
            override fun run() {
                val card = uiLockContainer ?: return
                val p = uiWindowParams ?: return

                val uptimeSec = (System.currentTimeMillis() - sessionStartTime) / 1000L
                val hours = uptimeSec / 3600
                val mins = (uptimeSec % 3600) / 60
                val secs = uptimeSec % 60
                tvUptime.text = String.format(Locale.US, "СЕССИЯ: %02d:%02d:%02d", hours, mins, secs)

                val shiftY = dp(60) + Random.nextInt(-8, 9)
                p.y = shiftY
                overlayWindowManager.updateViewSafe(card, p)

                mainHandler.postDelayed(this, 45000L)
            }
        }
        mainHandler.postDelayed(pixelShiftRunnable as Runnable, 45000L)
    }

    fun dismiss() {
        countdownRunnable?.let { mainHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        countdownRunnable = null
        pixelShiftRunnable?.let { mainHandler.removeCallbacks(it) }
        pixelShiftRunnable = null

        bgLockContainer?.let {
            overlayWindowManager.removeViewSafe(it)
            bgLockContainer = null
        }
        uiLockContainer?.let {
            overlayWindowManager.removeViewSafe(it)
            uiLockContainer = null
        }
        bgWindowParams = null
        uiWindowParams = null
        onLockDismissedListener?.invoke()
    }
}
```

```kotlin
package com.example.autotap.infrastructure.overlay.model

enum class PanelDisplayMode {
    EXPANDED,            // Полный двухстрочный режим (все инструменты)
    COMPACT_SINGLE_ROW,  // Компактная одиночная строка (быстрый доступ)
    MINI_BUBBLE          // Минималистичный плавающий бабл
}
```

```kotlin
    fun onPlayLongClicked()

    fun onRecordClicked()
```

📄 ФАЙЛ: app\src\main\java\com\example\autotap\infrastructure\overlay\panel\ControlPanelOverlay.kt
